<file path="src/components/library/SmartPDFViewer.tsx">
/**
 * SmartPDFViewer – PDF viewer with Adobe Embed API, citation highlights, and Ask the Tutor.
 *
 * - Renders PDF via Adobe PDF Embed SDK (script: documentcloud.adobe.com/view-sdk/main.js).
 * - Liquid Mode toggle (enableLinearization); highlights disabled when Liquid Mode is on.
 * - Citation overlay: accepts highlights as { page, bounds: [x,y,w,h] } (PDF coords, bottom-left)
 *   or { page, highlightBox: { top, left, width, height } } (percent). Converts PDF → viewport when needed.
 * - Ask the Tutor: on text selection, "Ask the Tutor" sends selected text to parent (e.g. Gemini Chat).
 */

import React, {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useId,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from 'react';
import { MessageCircle, AlertTriangle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import type { AdobeDCViewPreviewConfig, AdobeDCViewerAPIs } from '@/types/adobe-pdf-embed';
import { pdfBoundsToPercent } from '@/lib/utils/pdfCoordinates';

const ADOBE_VIEW_SDK_URL = 'https://documentcloud.adobe.com/view-sdk/main.js';
const ADOBE_READY_EVENT = 'adobe_dc_view_sdk.ready';

/** PDF coords: [x, y, width, height] with bottom-left origin (72 DPI typical). */
export type CitationBounds = [number, number, number, number];

/** Pre-converted percentage box (top-left origin). */
export interface CitationHighlightBox {
  top: number;
  left: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** Single citation: either raw PDF bounds or pre-converted highlightBox (percent). */
export interface CitationHighlight {
  page: number;
  /** PDF coords [x, y, w, h] – converted to percent using optional page dimensions. */
  bounds?: CitationBounds;
  /** Pre-converted percent (top, left, width, height). Used when present; otherwise bounds + pageDimensions. */
  highlightBox?: CitationHighlightBox;
}

/** Optional page dimensions for PDF→percent conversion (default 612×792). */
export interface PageDimensions {
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
}

export interface SmartPDFViewerProps {
  /** URL of the PDF to display. */
  readonly pdfUrl: string;
  /** Display name for the file. */
  readonly fileName?: string;
  /** Adobe PDF Embed API client ID (required for non-demo domains). */
  readonly clientId: string;
  /** Citation highlights: page + bounds (PDF coords) or highlightBox (percent). */
  readonly highlights?: CitationHighlight[];
  /** Optional page dimensions for bounds→percent (default 612×792). */
  readonly pageDimensions?: PageDimensions;
  /** Callback when user requests "Ask the Tutor" with selected text. */
  readonly onAskTutor?: (selectedText: string) => void;
  /** Optional class for the outer container. */
  readonly className?: string;
  /** Min height for the sizing container (responsive). */
  readonly minHeight?: string;
  /** Height for the sizing container (e.g. "70vh"). */
  readonly height?: string;
}

const DEFAULT_PAGE_WIDTH = 612;
const DEFAULT_PAGE_HEIGHT = 792;

/** Normalize all highlights to { page, highlightBox } (percent). */
function normalizeHighlights(
  highlights: CitationHighlight[],
  pageDimensions: PageDimensions
): Array<{ page: number; highlightBox: CitationHighlightBox }> {
  const w = pageDimensions.width ?? DEFAULT_PAGE_WIDTH;
  const h = pageDimensions.height ?? DEFAULT_PAGE_HEIGHT;
  return highlights.map((item) => {
    if (item.highlightBox) {
      return { page: item.page, highlightBox: item.highlightBox };
    }
    if (item.bounds && item.bounds.length >= 4) {
      const [x, y, bw, bh] = item.bounds;
      return {
        page: item.page,
        highlightBox: pdfBoundsToPercent(x, y, bw, bh, w, h) as CitationHighlightBox,
      };
    }
    return {
      page: item.page,
      highlightBox: { top: 0, left: 0, width: 100, height: 5 },
    };
  });
}

export function SmartPDFViewer({
  pdfUrl,
  fileName = 'document.pdf',
  clientId,
  highlights = [],
  pageDimensions = {},
  onAskTutor,
  className = '',
  minHeight = '400px',
  height = '70vh',
}: SmartPDFViewerProps) {
  const uniqueId = useId();
  const divId = `adobe-dc-view-${uniqueId.replaceAll(':', '-')}`;
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [sdkReady, setSdkReady] = useState(false);
  const [viewerReady, setViewerReady] = useState(false);
  const [liquidMode, setLiquidMode] = useState(false);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [askBubbleType, setAskBubbleType] = useState<'hint' | 'sent' | null>(null);
  const viewerPromiseRef = useRef<Promise<AdobeDCViewerAPIs> | null>(null);
  const askBubbleTimeoutRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const normalizedHighlights = useMemo(
    () => normalizeHighlights(highlights, pageDimensions),
    [highlights, pageDimensions]
  );

  const currentPageHighlights = useMemo(() => {
    if (liquidMode || normalizedHighlights.length === 0) return [];
    return normalizedHighlights.filter((h) => h.page === currentPage);
  }, [liquidMode, normalizedHighlights, currentPage]);

  // Load Adobe View SDK script once
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
    const existing = document.querySelector(`script[src="${ADOBE_VIEW_SDK_URL}"]`);
    if (existing) {
      if ((globalThis as Window & { adobe_dc_view_sdk_ready?: boolean }).adobe_dc_view_sdk_ready) {
        setSdkReady(true);
      } else {
        document.addEventListener(ADOBE_READY_EVENT, () => setSdkReady(true));
      }
      return;
    }
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = ADOBE_VIEW_SDK_URL;
    script.async = true;
    script.onload = () => {
      (globalThis as Window & { adobe_dc_view_sdk_ready?: boolean }).adobe_dc_view_sdk_ready = true;
      setSdkReady(true);
    };
    script.onerror = () => setError('Failed to load Adobe PDF Embed SDK.');
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  }, []);

  // Initialize viewer when SDK is ready and container exists
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sdkReady || !clientId || !containerRef.current) return;
    const AdobeDC = (globalThis as Window).AdobeDC;
    if (!AdobeDC) {
      setError('Adobe PDF Embed SDK not available.');
      return;
    }

    setError(null);
    const view = new AdobeDC.View({ clientId, divId });

    const previewConfig: AdobeDCViewPreviewConfig = {
      embedMode: 'SIZED_CONTAINER',
      showDownloadPDF: true,
      showPrintPDF: true,
      showPageControls: true,
      enableLinearization: liquidMode,
    };

    const promise = view.previewFile(
      {
        content: { location: { url: pdfUrl } },
        metaData: { fileName },
      },
      previewConfig
    ) as Promise<AdobeDCViewerAPIs>;

    viewerPromiseRef.current = promise;
    promise
      .then(() => {
        setViewerReady(true);
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load PDF.');
        setViewerReady(false);
      });

    return () => {
      viewerPromiseRef.current = null;
      setViewerReady(false);
    };
  }, [sdkReady, clientId, divId, pdfUrl, fileName, liquidMode]);

  // Re-init viewer when liquidMode toggles (enableLinearization is a preview config)
  // Already in dependency array above

  // Poll current page when viewer is ready
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!viewerReady || !viewerPromiseRef.current) return;
    const interval = setInterval(async () => {
      try {
        const viewer = viewerPromiseRef.current ? await viewerPromiseRef.current : undefined;
        if (!viewer?.getAPIs) return;
        const apis = await viewer.getAPIs();
        const page = await apis.getCurrentPage?.();
        if (typeof page === 'number') setCurrentPage(page);
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }, 500);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [viewerReady]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (askBubbleTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(askBubbleTimeoutRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleAskTutor = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!onAskTutor) return;
    if (askBubbleTimeoutRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(askBubbleTimeoutRef.current);
      askBubbleTimeoutRef.current = null;
    }
    try {
      const viewer = viewerPromiseRef.current ? await viewerPromiseRef.current : undefined;
      if (!viewer?.getAPIs) {
        setAskBubbleType('hint');
        askBubbleTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => setAskBubbleType(null), 3000);
        return;
      }
      const apis = await viewer.getAPIs();
      const result = await apis.getSelectedContent?.();
      if (result?.data?.trim()) {
        onAskTutor(result.data.trim());
        setAskBubbleType('sent');
        askBubbleTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => setAskBubbleType(null), 2000);
      } else {
        setAskBubbleType('hint');
        askBubbleTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => setAskBubbleType(null), 3000);
      }
    } catch {
      setAskBubbleType('hint');
      askBubbleTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => setAskBubbleType(null), 3000);
    }
  }, [onAskTutor]);

  return (
    <div className={`flex flex-col rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)] ${className}`}>
      {/* Toolbar: Liquid Mode + Ask the Tutor */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 border-b border-[var(--color-border)] bg-[var(--color-bg-primary)]">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={liquidMode}
            onChange={(e) => setLiquidMode(e.target.checked)}
            className="rounded border-[var(--color-border)] text-[var(--color-accent)] focus:ring-[var(--color-accent)]"
          />
          <span className="text-sm text-[var(--color-text-primary)]">Liquid Mode (mobile-friendly)</span>
        </label>
        {onAskTutor && (
          <div className="relative">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleAskTutor}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[var(--color-accent)] text-white text-sm font-medium hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity"
            >
              <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden />
              Ask the Tutor
            </button>
            {askBubbleType && (
              <div
                className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)] border border-[var(--color-border)] text-xs text-[var(--color-text-muted)] shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap"
                aria-live="polite"
                aria-atomic="true"
              >
                {askBubbleType === 'sent'
                  ? 'Sent to tutor. Ask your question in the chat.'
                  : 'Select text in the PDF, then click "Ask the Tutor" to send it to the chat.'}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Liquid Mode: citation highlights not supported */}
      {liquidMode && highlights.length > 0 && (
        <div
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-amber-500/10 border-b border-amber-500/20 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200 text-sm"
          role="alert"
        >
          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
          <span>
            Citation highlights are disabled in Liquid Mode because layout and coordinates differ. Turn off Liquid Mode to see highlights.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-red-500/10 border-b border-red-500/20 text-red-700 dark:text-red-300 text-sm"
          role="alert"
        >
          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* SizingContainer: responsive height per Adobe View Configurations */}
      <div
        ref={containerRef}
        className="relative flex-1 w-full min-w-0"
        style={{ minHeight, height }}
      >
        {!sdkReady && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)]">
            <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-[var(--color-text-muted)]" aria-hidden />
          </div>
        )}
        <div
          id={divId}
          className="w-full h-full"
          style={{ minHeight, height }}
          aria-label="PDF document viewer"
        />

        {/* Highlight overlay: only when not Liquid Mode and we have highlights for current page */}
        {viewerReady && currentPageHighlights.length > 0 && (
          <div
            className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none"
            aria-hidden
          >
            {currentPageHighlights.map((item, idx) => (
              <div
                key={`${item.page}-${idx}`}
                className="absolute border-2 border-amber-500/80 bg-amber-400/20 rounded-sm"
                style={{
                  top: `${item.highlightBox.top}%`,
                  left: `${item.highlightBox.left}%`,
                  width: `${item.highlightBox.width}%`,
                  height: `${item.highlightBox.height}%`,
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/settings/ShortcutSettings.tsx">
/**
 * ShortcutSettings - Keyboard Shortcut Configuration Panel
 *
 * Allows users to view and customize keyboard shortcuts for the application.
 * Features:
 * - Visual display of all shortcuts
 * - Click-to-rebind interface
 * - Key conflict detection
 * - Reset to defaults
 * - Dark mode theme
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { RotateCcw, Keyboard, AlertCircle, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  useShortcutContext,
  type ShortcutAction,
  formatKeyForDisplay,
  getActionDisplayName,
  isKeyConflict,
} from '../../context/ShortcutContext';

interface ShortcutSettingsProps {
  /** Optional callback when shortcuts are updated */
  onUpdate?: () => void;
  /** Optional CSS class for container */
  className?: string;
}

export const ShortcutSettings: React.FC<ShortcutSettingsProps> = ({ onUpdate, className = '' }) => {
  const { shortcuts, updateShortcut, resetShortcuts } = useShortcutContext();
  const [listeningFor, setListeningFor] = useState<ShortcutAction | null>(null);
  const [conflictWarning, setConflictWarning] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // List of all actions in display order
  const actions: ShortcutAction[] = [
    'FLIP_CARD',
    'MARK_CORRECT',
    'MARK_WRONG',
    'NEXT_QUESTION',
    'PREV_QUESTION',
    'PLAY_AUDIO',
    'TOGGLE_STATS',
  ];

  // Handle key capture when listening
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listeningFor) return;

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();

      // Cancel on Escape
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        setListeningFor(null);
        return;
      }

      const newKey = event.key;

      // Check for conflicts
      const conflictAction = isKeyConflict(shortcuts, newKey, listeningFor);
      if (conflictAction) {
        setConflictWarning(
          `Key "${formatKeyForDisplay(newKey)}" is already assigned to ${getActionDisplayName(conflictAction)}`
        );
        setTimeout(() => setConflictWarning(null), 3000);
        setListeningFor(null);
        return;
      }

      // Update the shortcut
      updateShortcut(listeningFor, newKey);
      setListeningFor(null);

      // Show success message
      setSuccessMessage(
        `${getActionDisplayName(listeningFor)} set to "${formatKeyForDisplay(newKey)}"`
      );
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 2000);

      // Notify parent component
      if (onUpdate) {
        onUpdate();
      }
    };

    // Add listener
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);

    // Cleanup
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
    };
  }, [listeningFor, shortcuts, updateShortcut, onUpdate]);

  // Handle click outside to cancel listening
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listeningFor) return;

    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setListeningFor(null);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, [listeningFor]);

  // Handle reset to defaults
  const handleReset = () => {
    resetShortcuts();
    setSuccessMessage('Shortcuts reset to defaults');
    setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 2000);

    if (onUpdate) {
      onUpdate();
    }
  };

  // Start listening for a key
  const startListening = (action: ShortcutAction) => {
    setListeningFor(action);
    setConflictWarning(null);
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className={`bg-[var(--color-bg-primary)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] p-6 ${className}`}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Keyboard className="w-6 h-6 text-[var(--color-accent)]" />
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--color-text-primary)]">
            Keyboard Shortcuts
          </h2>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={handleReset}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)] hover:bg-[var(--color-bg-tertiary)] text-[var(--color-text-muted)] rounded-lg transition-colors border border-[var(--color-border)]"
        >
          <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">Reset to Defaults</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Instructions */}
      <p className="text-[var(--color-text-muted)] text-sm mb-6">
        Click any shortcut button to rebind it. Press{' '}
        <kbd className="px-2 py-1 bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)] rounded border border-[var(--color-border)] text-xs">
          Escape
        </kbd>{' '}
        to cancel.
      </p>

      {/* Success Message */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {successMessage && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 px-4 py-3 bg-data-pass/10 border border-data-pass/30 rounded-lg"
          >
            <Check className="w-5 h-5 text-data-pass" />
            <span className="text-data-pass text-sm">{successMessage}</span>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Conflict Warning */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {conflictWarning && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 px-4 py-3 bg-data-fail/10 border border-data-fail/30 rounded-lg"
          >
            <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-data-fail" />
            <span className="text-data-fail text-sm">{conflictWarning}</span>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Shortcut List */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {actions.map((action) => {
          const currentKey = shortcuts[action];
          const isListening = listeningFor === action;
          const displayName = getActionDisplayName(action);
          const displayKey = formatKeyForDisplay(currentKey);

          return (
            <motion.div
              key={action}
              layout
              className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)] rounded-lg border border-[var(--color-border)] hover:border-[var(--color-border-strong)] transition-colors"
            >
              {/* Action Name */}
              <div className="flex-1">
                <h3 className="text-[var(--color-text-primary)] font-medium">{displayName}</h3>
                <p className="text-[var(--color-text-muted)] text-xs mt-1">
                  {getActionDescription(action)}
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Key Button */}
              <button
                onClick={() => startListening(action)}
                disabled={isListening}
                className={`
                  min-w-[120px] px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-bold transition-all
                  ${
                    isListening
                      ? 'bg-[var(--color-accent)] text-[var(--color-text-inverse)] border-2 border-[var(--color-accent)] animate-pulse'
                      : 'bg-[var(--color-bg-tertiary)] text-[var(--color-text-primary)] border-2 border-[var(--color-border)] hover:border-[var(--color-accent)] hover:bg-[var(--color-bg-tertiary)]'
                  }
                `}
              >
                {isListening ? (
                  <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-[var(--color-text-inverse)] rounded-full animate-ping" />
                    Press any key...
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="tracking-wider">[ {displayKey} ]</span>
                )}
              </button>
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Footer Note */}
      <div className="mt-6 p-4 bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)]/70 rounded-lg border border-[var(--color-border)]">
        <p className="text-[var(--color-text-muted)] text-xs">
          <strong className="text-[var(--color-text-secondary)]">Note:</strong> Shortcuts are
          automatically saved to your browser's local storage. They will not trigger when you are
          typing in text fields.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ============================================================================
// HELPER FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Get a brief description for each action
 */
function getActionDescription(action: ShortcutAction): string {
  const descriptions: Record<ShortcutAction, string> = {
    FLIP_CARD: 'Reveal the answer on a flashcard',
    MARK_CORRECT: 'Mark current question as correct',
    MARK_WRONG: 'Mark current question as incorrect',
    NEXT_QUESTION: 'Navigate to the next question',
    PREV_QUESTION: 'Navigate to the previous question',
    PLAY_AUDIO: 'Play audio pronunciation or explanation',
    TOGGLE_STATS: 'Show/hide session statistics overlay',
  };

  return descriptions[action];
}

// ============================================================================
// COMPACT VERSION (Optional Alternative)
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Compact version of ShortcutSettings for smaller UI spaces
 */
export const ShortcutSettingsCompact: React.FC<ShortcutSettingsProps> = ({
  onUpdate,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const { shortcuts, updateShortcut, resetShortcuts } = useShortcutContext();
  const [listeningFor, setListeningFor] = useState<ShortcutAction | null>(null);

  const actions: ShortcutAction[] = [
    'FLIP_CARD',
    'MARK_CORRECT',
    'MARK_WRONG',
    'NEXT_QUESTION',
    'PREV_QUESTION',
    'PLAY_AUDIO',
    'TOGGLE_STATS',
  ];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listeningFor) return;

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        setListeningFor(null);
        return;
      }
      updateShortcut(listeningFor, event.key);
      setListeningFor(null);
      if (onUpdate) onUpdate();
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
  }, [listeningFor, updateShortcut, onUpdate]);

  return (
    <div className={`bg-[var(--color-bg-secondary)] rounded-lg p-4 ${className}`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <h3 className="text-[var(--color-text-primary)] font-semibold text-sm">Shortcuts</h3>
        <button
          onClick={resetShortcuts}
          className="text-xs text-[var(--color-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--color-text-secondary)]"
        >
          Reset
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {actions.map((action) => (
          <div key={action} className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
            <span className="text-[var(--color-text-muted)]">{getActionDisplayName(action)}</span>
            <button
              onClick={() => setListeningFor(action)}
              className={`
                px-3 py-1 rounded font-mono text-xs
                ${
                  listeningFor === action
                    ? 'bg-[var(--color-accent)] text-[var(--color-text-inverse)]'
                    : 'bg-[var(--color-bg-tertiary)] text-[var(--color-text-secondary)] hover:bg-[var(--color-bg-tertiary)]'
                }
              `}
            >
              {listeningFor === action ? 'Press key...' : formatKeyForDisplay(shortcuts[action])}
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ShortcutSettings;

<file path="src/context/ShortcutContext.tsx">
/**
 * ShortcutContext - Customizable Keyboard Shortcut System
 *
 * Provides a centralized system for managing keyboard shortcuts across the app.
 * Features:
 * - Customizable key bindings
 * - Persistent storage in localStorage
 * - Global shortcut hook with auto-cleanup
 * - Input field detection (ignores shortcuts when typing)
 * - Easy to extend with new actions
 */

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';

// ============================================================================
// TYPES
// ============================================================================

export type ShortcutAction =
  | 'FLIP_CARD'
  | 'MARK_CORRECT'
  | 'MARK_WRONG'
  | 'NEXT_QUESTION'
  | 'PREV_QUESTION'
  | 'PLAY_AUDIO'
  | 'TOGGLE_STATS';

export type ShortcutMap = Record<ShortcutAction, string>;

// ============================================================================
// DEFAULT SHORTCUTS
// ============================================================================

export const DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS: ShortcutMap = {
  FLIP_CARD: ' ', // Space
  MARK_CORRECT: '1', // Number 1
  MARK_WRONG: '2', // Number 2
  NEXT_QUESTION: 'ArrowRight',
  PREV_QUESTION: 'ArrowLeft',
  PLAY_AUDIO: 'p',
  TOGGLE_STATS: 's', // S for stats
};

// ============================================================================
// CONTEXT
// ============================================================================

interface ShortcutContextValue {
  shortcuts: ShortcutMap;
  updateShortcut: (action: ShortcutAction, newKey: string) => void;
  resetShortcuts: () => void;
  getKeyForAction: (action: ShortcutAction) => string;
  getActionForKey: (key: string) => ShortcutAction | null;
}

const ShortcutContext = createContext<ShortcutContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

const STORAGE_KEY = 'panceai_shortcuts';

// ============================================================================
// PROVIDER
// ============================================================================

export const ShortcutProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [shortcuts, setShortcuts] = useState<ShortcutMap>(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS);

  // Load shortcuts from localStorage on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (saved) {
        const parsedShortcuts = JSON.parse(saved) as Partial<ShortcutMap>;

        // Merge with defaults to handle new actions added in future updates
        const mergedShortcuts = { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS };

        // Override with saved values
        for (const action in parsedShortcuts) {
          if (action in DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS) {
            mergedShortcuts[action as ShortcutAction] = parsedShortcuts[action as ShortcutAction]!;
          }
        }

        setShortcuts(mergedShortcuts);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('[ShortcutContext] Failed to load shortcuts from localStorage:', error);
      setShortcuts(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS);
    }
  }, []);

  // Update a single shortcut
  const updateShortcut = useCallback((action: ShortcutAction, newKey: string) => {
    setShortcuts((prev) => {
      const updated = { ...prev, [action]: newKey };

      // Save to localStorage
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('[ShortcutContext] Failed to save shortcuts to localStorage:', error);
      }

      return updated;
    });
  }, []);

  // Reset all shortcuts to defaults
  const resetShortcuts = useCallback(() => {
    setShortcuts(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS);

    try {
      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('[ShortcutContext] Failed to reset shortcuts:', error);
    }
  }, []);

  // Get key for a specific action
  const getKeyForAction = useCallback(
    (action: ShortcutAction): string => {
      return shortcuts[action];
    },
    [shortcuts]
  );

  // Get action for a specific key (reverse lookup)
  const getActionForKey = useCallback(
    (key: string): ShortcutAction | null => {
      const entry = Object.entries(shortcuts).find(([_, value]) => value === key);
      return entry ? (entry[0] as ShortcutAction) : null;
    },
    [shortcuts]
  );

  const value: ShortcutContextValue = {
    shortcuts,
    updateShortcut,
    resetShortcuts,
    getKeyForAction,
    getActionForKey,
  };

  return <ShortcutContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ShortcutContext.Provider>;
};

// ============================================================================
// CUSTOM HOOK - useShortcut
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Hook for registering a keyboard shortcut
 *
 * @param action - The shortcut action to listen for
 * @param callback - Function to call when the shortcut is triggered
 * @param options - Optional configuration
 *
 * @example
 * useShortcut('FLIP_CARD', () => {
 *   console.log('Space pressed!');
 *   flipCard();
 * });
 */
export function useShortcut(
  action: ShortcutAction,
  callback: () => void,
  options?: {
    enabled?: boolean; // Whether the shortcut is currently active (default: true)
    preventDefault?: boolean; // Whether to prevent default browser behavior (default: true)
  }
): void {
  const context = useContext(ShortcutContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useShortcut must be used within a ShortcutProvider');
  }

  const { shortcuts } = context;
  const { enabled = true, preventDefault = true } = options || {};

  useEffect(() => {
    // Don't attach listener if disabled
    if (!enabled) return;

    const key = shortcuts[action];
    if (!key) return;

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Ignore if user is typing in an input field
      const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
      const isInputField =
        target.tagName === 'INPUT' || target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || target.isContentEditable;

      if (isInputField) return;

      // Check if the pressed key matches the shortcut
      if (event.key === key) {
        if (preventDefault) {
          event.preventDefault();
        }

        callback();
      }
    };

    // Add global keydown listener
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);

    // Cleanup on unmount
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [action, callback, shortcuts, enabled, preventDefault]);
}

// ============================================================================
// CONTEXT HOOK
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Hook to access the shortcut context
 *
 * @example
 * const { shortcuts, updateShortcut, resetShortcuts } = useShortcutContext();
 */
export function useShortcutContext(): ShortcutContextValue {
  const context = useContext(ShortcutContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useShortcutContext must be used within a ShortcutProvider');
  }

  return context;
}

// ============================================================================
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Format a key for display (e.g., 'ArrowRight' -> 'Arrow Right', ' ' -> 'Space')
 */
export function formatKeyForDisplay(key: string): string {
  const keyMap: Record<string, string> = {
    ' ': 'Space',
    ArrowUp: 'Arrow Up',
    ArrowDown: 'Arrow Down',
    ArrowLeft: 'Arrow Left',
    ArrowRight: 'Arrow Right',
    Enter: 'Enter',
    Escape: 'Escape',
    Tab: 'Tab',
    Backspace: 'Backspace',
    Delete: 'Delete',
  };

  return keyMap[key] || key.toUpperCase();
}

/**
 * Check if a key is already assigned to another action
 */
export function isKeyConflict(
  shortcuts: ShortcutMap,
  key: string,
  excludeAction?: ShortcutAction
): ShortcutAction | null {
  const entries = Object.entries(shortcuts) as [ShortcutAction, string][];

  for (const [action, assignedKey] of entries) {
    if (assignedKey === key && action !== excludeAction) {
      return action;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

/**
 * Get a human-readable action name
 */
export function getActionDisplayName(action: ShortcutAction): string {
  const displayNames: Record<ShortcutAction, string> = {
    FLIP_CARD: 'Flip Card',
    MARK_CORRECT: 'Mark Correct',
    MARK_WRONG: 'Mark Wrong',
    NEXT_QUESTION: 'Next Question',
    PREV_QUESTION: 'Previous Question',
    PLAY_AUDIO: 'Play Audio',
    TOGGLE_STATS: 'Toggle Stats Overlay',
  };

  return displayNames[action];
}

/**
 * Get conditions by category for targeted generation
 */
export function getConditionsByCategory(category: string): string[] {
  // This is a simplified version - could be enhanced with explicit categorization
  return LAB_DRIVEN_CONDITIONS.filter((condition) => {
    const lowerCondition = condition.toLowerCase();
    const lowerCategory = category.toLowerCase();

    switch (lowerCategory) {
      case 'hematology':
        return (
          lowerCondition.includes('anemia') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('leukemia') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('thrombocyt') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('hemophilia') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('polycythemia') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('sickle') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('thalassemia') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('myeloma')
        );
      case 'metabolic':
      case 'endocrine':
        return (
          lowerCondition.includes('diabetes') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('thyroid') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('acidosis') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('alkalosis') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('addison') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('cushing') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('calcium') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('parathyroid')
        );
      case 'renal':
        return (
          lowerCondition.includes('kidney') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('renal') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('nephro') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('kalemia') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('natremia') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('glomerulo')
        );
      case 'hepatic':
        return (
          lowerCondition.includes('hepat') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('liver') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('cirrhosis') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('cholang')
        );
      case 'cardiac':
        return (
          lowerCondition.includes('myocardial') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('heart') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('cardiac') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('angina') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('embolism') ||
          lowerCondition.includes('cardiomyopathy')
        );
      default:
        return false;
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Get random condition from the database
 */
export function getRandomCondition(): string {
  const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * LAB_DRIVEN_CONDITIONS.length);
  return LAB_DRIVEN_CONDITIONS[idx] ?? '';
}

/**
 * Get random conditions (avoiding duplicates in the same batch)
 */
export function getRandomConditions(count: number): string[] {
  const shuffled = [...LAB_DRIVEN_CONDITIONS].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
  return shuffled.slice(0, Math.min(count, LAB_DRIVEN_CONDITIONS.length));
}
</file>

<file path="src/lib/conditionSearch.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

import { searchConditions } from './conditionSearch.ts';

describe('conditionSearch', () => {
  it('fuzzy search matches minor typos', async () => {
    const results = await searchConditions('fibrilation');
    const top = results[0];
    expect(top).toBeDefined();
    expect(top?.condition).toBe('Atrial Fibrillation');
  });

  it('returns empty array on blank query', async () => {
    const results = await searchConditions('   ');
    expect(results).toEqual([]);
  });
});

<file path="src/lib/unifiedSearch.test.ts">
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { unifiedSearch, type UnifiedSearchResult } from './unifiedSearch';

describe('unifiedSearch', () => {
  it('returns empty array on blank query', async () => {
    expect(await unifiedSearch('')).toEqual([]);
    expect(await unifiedSearch('   ')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('prioritizes conditions over drugs with condition boost', async () => {
    // Search for "pneumonia" - should show condition results first
    const results = (await unifiedSearch('pneumonia')) as UnifiedSearchResult[];
    expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // Find first condition and first drug if they exist
    const firstCondition = results.find((r) => r.type === 'condition');
    const firstDrug = results.find((r) => r.type === 'drug');

    if (firstCondition && firstDrug) {
      const conditionIndex = results.indexOf(firstCondition);
      const drugIndex = results.indexOf(firstDrug);

      // Condition should appear before drug
      expect(conditionIndex).toBeLessThan(drugIndex);
    }
  });

  it('finds both conditions and drugs for general terms', async () => {
    const results = (await unifiedSearch('diabetes')) as UnifiedSearchResult[];
    expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // Should have at least some results
    const hasConditions = results.some((r) => r.type === 'condition');
    expect(hasConditions).toBe(true);
  });

  it('finds drugs by brand name', async () => {
    const results = (await unifiedSearch('Prozac')) as UnifiedSearchResult[];
    expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const hasDrugs = results.some((r) => r.type === 'drug');
    expect(hasDrugs).toBe(true);
  });

  it('properly formats result names with capitalization', async () => {
    const results = (await unifiedSearch('neomycin')) as UnifiedSearchResult[];
    expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const drugResult = results.find(
      (r) => r.type === 'drug' && r.name.toLowerCase().includes('neomycin')
    );
    if (drugResult) {
      // Should be capitalized, not lowercase
      expect(drugResult.name).not.toBe('neomycin');
      expect(drugResult.name.charAt(0)).toBe(drugResult.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase());
    }
  });

  it('limits results to 20 items', async () => {
    const results = (await unifiedSearch('a')) as UnifiedSearchResult[];
    expect(results.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(20);
  });

  it('includes proper subtitle information', async () => {
    const results = (await unifiedSearch('aspirin')) as UnifiedSearchResult[];
    expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const result = results[0];
    expect(result).toBeDefined();
    expect(result?.subtitle).toBeDefined();
    expect((result?.subtitle ?? '').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

model ConfusionPair {
  id     String @id @default(cuid())

  mistakenFor   String

  count          Int      @default(1)

  createdAt      DateTime @default(now())

  // Legacy Condition relations
  realConditionId   String?
  mistakenForId     String?
  RealCondition     Condition? @relation("ConfusionPairReal", fields: [realConditionId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  MistakenCondition Condition? @relation("ConfusionPairMistaken", fields: [mistakenForId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  // New MedicalContent-linked relations for precise comparisons
  correctConditionId  String?
  selectedConditionId String?
  CorrectCondition    MedicalContent? @relation("CorrectCondition", fields: [correctConditionId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  SelectedCondition   MedicalContent? @relation("SelectedCondition", fields: [selectedConditionId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  User User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([userId, realCondition, mistakenFor])
  @@unique([userId, correctConditionId, selectedConditionId])
  @@index([mistakenForId])
  @@index([realCondition])
  @@index([realConditionId])
  @@index([correctConditionId])
  @@index([selectedConditionId])
  @@index([userId])
  @@index([userId, correctConditionId])
  @@index([userId, realCondition])
}

  mostDangerous             String[]                    @default([])
  oftenMissed               String[]                    @default([])
  panceYield                Int?
  reassuringFeatures        String[]                    @default([])
  redFlags                  String[]                    @default([])
  typicalPresentation       String?
  // New FK for deep relations
  primaryConditionId        String?
  PrimaryCondition          Condition?                  @relation(fields: [primaryConditionId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  DifferentialConditionLink DifferentialConditionLink[]

  @@index([category])
  @@index([isEmergency])
  @@index([panceYield])
  @@index([primaryConditionId])
}

/// Per-user Gemini context cache for "Chat with your Library" and student-context tutor.
/// source: 'upload' | 'drive' | 'student-context'

  id               String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId           String
  displayName      String
  geminiCacheName  String   // e.g. cachedContents/xxx
  geminiFileIds    String[] @default([]) // file names/URIs used in cache (optional)
  expiresAt        DateTime
  source           String   // 'upload' | 'drive' | 'student-context'
  createdAt        DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime
  User             User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([userId, source])
  @@index([userId, expiresAt])
}

model ContrastiveSet {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  symptom      String // "Chest Pain", "Dyspnea", "Abdominal Pain"
  conditionIds String[] // Array of 5 MedicalContent IDs

  // Pre-computed distinguishing features
  distinguishers Json // { "MI": ["troponin", "ST elevation"], "PE": ["pleuritic", "Wells score"] }

  // Metadata
  system     String? // Primary system (CARDIO, PULM, etc.)
  difficulty String  @default("medium")
  highYield  Boolean @default(false)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  ContrastiveDrillAttempt ContrastiveDrillAttempt[]

  @@index([symptom])
  @@index([system])
}

model ContrastiveDrillAttempt {
  id     String @id @default(cuid())
  userId String
  setId  String // ContrastiveSet ID

  // Performance tracking
  questionsAsked   Int      @default(0)
  correctAnswers   Int      @default(0)
  conditionsMissed String[] // Which conditions were confused
  timeSpentMs      Int?

  completedAt DateTime @default(now())

  user User           @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  set  ContrastiveSet @relation(fields: [setId], references: [id])

  @@index([userId, setId])
}

model MediaAsset {
  id                  String                @id

  // Photo Drill fields
  difficulty          String                @default("medium") /// easy, medium, hard
  sourceQuality       String                @default("high") /// low, medium, high
  modality            String? /// Normalized: ecg, derm, radiology
  explanation         String? /// Explanation shown after answering
  // Annotation tracking for quiz vs reference images
  isAnnotated         Boolean               @default(false) /// Has arrows/labels that give away diagnosis
  usageType           String                @default("quiz") /// quiz = clean image, reference = annotated, both = either
  // Relations
  Condition           Condition?            @relation(fields: [conditionId], references: [id])
  ImagingStudy        ImagingStudy?         @relation(fields: [imagingStudyId], references: [id])
  MedicalContent      MedicalContent?       @relation(fields: [medicalContentId], references: [id])
  Drug                Drug?                 @relation(fields: [drugId], references: [id])
  Procedure           Procedure?            @relation(fields: [procedureId], references: [id])
  PhysicalExamFinding PhysicalExamFinding?  @relation(fields: [findingId], references: [id])
  AnatomyStructure    AnatomyStructure?     @relation(fields: [anatomyId], references: [id])
  LabTest             LabTest?              @relation(fields: [labTestId], references: [id])
  MedicalContentMedia MedicalContentMedia[]

  @@index([approvalStatus])
  @@index([approvedAt])
  @@index([conditionId])
  @@index([difficulty])
  @@index([filename])
  @@index([folder])
  @@index([imagingStudyId])
  @@index([medicalContentId])
  @@index([drugId])
  @@index([procedureId])
  @@index([findingId])
  @@index([anatomyId])
  @@index([labTestId])
  @@index([mediaType])
  @@index([modality])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([tags])
  @@index([type])
  @@index([uploadedBy])
}

/// This model or at least one of its fields has comments in the database, and requires an additional setup for migrations: Read more: https://pris.ly/d/database-comments
model MedicalContent {
  id                    String                   @id
  conditionId           String                   @unique
  system                String
  subcategory           String
  condition             String
  status                String                   @default("draft")
  version               Int                      @default(1)
  createdAt             DateTime                 @default(now())
  createdBy             String
  updatedAt             DateTime
  updatedBy             String
  publishedAt           DateTime?
  publishedBy           String?
  approvedAt            DateTime?
  approvedBy            String?
  content               Json?
  relatedSystems        String[]                 @default([])
  parent_category       String?
  buzzwords             String[]                 @default([])
  complications         String?
  diagnostics           String?
  differentialDiagnosis String?
  epidemiology          String?
  etiology              String?
  overview              String?
  pathophysiology       String?
  physicalExam          String?
  prognosis             String?
  riskFactors           String?
  symptoms              String?
  treatment             String?
  classic_triad         Json?
  clinical_pearls       Json?
  first_line_rx         String?
  gold_standard_dx      String?
  age_demographic       Json?
  best_initial_test     String?
  classic_patient       String?
  differentials         Json?
  disposition           String?
  gender_bias           String?
  guidelines            String?
  image_query           String?
  mnemonic              String?
  pance_yield           Int?
  patient_education     String?
  prevention            String?
  synonyms              Json?
  search_vector         Unsupported("tsvector")?

  // Hierarchy fields
  parentId         String?
  relationshipType String? // "manifestation" | "subtype" | "complication" | "variant"
  canonicalName    String?

  // Hierarchy relations
  parent   MedicalContent?  @relation("ConditionHierarchy", fields: [parentId], references: [id])
  children MedicalContent[] @relation("ConditionHierarchy")

  // Inverse relations from junction tables with medicalContentId
  ContentAuditLog             ContentAuditLog[]
  ContentVersion              ContentVersion[]
  MedicalContentMedia         MedicalContentMedia[]
  AnatomyConditionLinks       AnatomyConditionLink[]
  DrugConditionLinks          DrugConditionLink[]
  ECGConditionLinks           ECGConditionLink[]
  FindingConditionLinks       FindingConditionLink[]
  ImagingConditionLinks       ImagingConditionLink[]
  LabConditionLinks           LabConditionLink[]
  PhysiologyConditionLinks    PhysiologyConditionLink[]
  ProcedureConditionLinks     ProcedureConditionLink[]
  TreatmentConditionLinks     TreatmentConditionLink[]
  DifferentialConditionLinks  DifferentialConditionLink[]
  AntibioticConditionLinks    AntibioticConditionLink[]
  VitalSignConditionLinks     VitalSignConditionLink[]
  ScoringSystemConditionLinks ScoringSystemConditionLink[]
  PearlConditionLinks         PearlConditionLink[]
  // Inverse relations from direct entity models with medicalContentId
  ClinicalPearls              ClinicalPearl[]
  MediaAssets                 MediaAsset[]
  Questions                   Question[]
  PreGeneratedQuestions       PreGeneratedQuestion[]
  QuestionSeeds               QuestionSeed[]
  SavedQuestions              SavedQuestion[]
  WeaknessPatterns            WeaknessPattern[]
  UserProgress                UserProgress[]
  ContentStatistics           ContentStatistics[]
  ConfusionPairsCorrect       ConfusionPair[]              @relation("CorrectCondition")
  ConfusionPairsSelected      ConfusionPair[]              @relation("SelectedCondition")
  ReviewLog                   ReviewLog[]

  @@index([conditionId])
  @@index([createdBy])
  @@index([relatedSystems])
  @@index([parentId])
  @@index([canonicalName])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([system])
  @@index([updatedAt])
  @@index([system, pance_yield])
  @@index([parent_category])
  @@index([system, parent_category])
  @@index([system, subcategory, parent_category])
  @@index([status, conditionId])
}

// =============================================================================
// UserProgress: DEPRECATED - Replaced by ReviewLog for performance (500ms → 5ms)
// The reviewHistory Json[] field is a major anti-pattern for large datasets

  id             String         @id @default(uuid())

  reviewHistory  Json[]         @default([])
  totalAttempts  Int            @default(0)

  lastReviewAt   DateTime?
  nextReviewAt   DateTime?
  createdAt      DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime       @updatedAt
  User           User           @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  MedicalContent MedicalContent @relation(fields: [conditionId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([userId, conditionId])
  @@index([userId, nextReviewAt])
  @@index([conditionId])
  @@index([userId, conditionId])
}

// =============================================================================
// REVIEWLOG: High-Performance Time-Series Table for FSRS v6 Optimization
// Replaces UserProgress.reviewHistory Json[] for 100x speedup (500ms → 5ms)
// Algorithm-facing fields: rating, state, due_date, review_date, duration, review_type
// =============================================================================

model ReviewLog {
  id     String @id @default(cuid())
  userId String

  // Question/Content identification
  conditionId      String? // MedicalContent.conditionId
  medicalContentId String? // Direct FK to MedicalContent
  questionId       String? // Source question ID (polymorphic: Question | PreGeneratedQuestion | QuestionSeed)
  questionType     String? // 'pre_generated' | 'question' | 'seed'
  questionFkId     String? // FK to Question when questionType='question' (enables proper relation)

  // === Algorithm-facing fields (exact names for FSRS integration) ===
  rating     Int       @map("grade") // FSRS rating: 1 (Again), 2 (Hard), 3 (Good), 4 (Easy)
  state      Int       // 0=New, 1=Learning, 2=Review, 3=Relearning
  due_date   DateTime? @map("scheduledAt") // When this review was scheduled
  review_date DateTime @default(now()) @map("reviewedAt") // When reviewed
  duration   Int?      @map("responseTimeMs") // Milliseconds to answer
  review_type String   @default("real") // "real" (MAIN) vs "cram" (CRAM/RAPID_RECALL) - filter for algorithm

  // FSRS v6 extended state (for optimizer)
  stability      Float  // S value at time of review
  difficulty     Float  // D value at time of review
  retrievability Float? // R value at time of review (calculated)
  elapsedDays    Float? // Days since last review
  wasCorrect     Boolean // Actual correctness

  // Context
  sessionType SessionType @default(MAIN) // Kept for backward compat; maps to review_type
  sessionId   String? // FK to StudySession
  attemptId   String? // FK to QuestionAttempt
  deviceType  String? // 'mobile' | 'tablet' | 'desktop'
  system      String? // Normalized system code (e.g., 'CV', 'PULM')
  telemetry   Json? // Compact behavioral metrics

  // Metadata
  fsrsVersion String   @default("6.0")
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())

  // Relations
  User           User?           @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  MedicalContent MedicalContent? @relation(fields: [medicalContentId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  Question       Question?       @relation(fields: [questionFkId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  // === 8 CRITICAL INDEXES FOR FSRS OPTIMIZER ===
  @@index([userId, review_date])
  @@index([userId, conditionId, review_date])
  @@index([userId, system, review_date])
  @@index([review_date])
  @@index([conditionId, review_date])
  @@index([medicalContentId])
  @@index([userId, state, review_date])
  @@index([userId, review_type, review_date]) // CRITICAL: Fast filtering of "real" vs "cram"
  @@index([userId, rating, review_date])
  @@index([questionFkId])
}

  updatedAt        DateTime       @default(now())
  MedicalContent   MedicalContent @relation(fields: [medicalContentId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  MediaAsset       MediaAsset     @relation(fields: [mediaAssetId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([medicalContentId, mediaAssetId])
  @@index([medicalContentId])
  @@index([mediaAssetId])
}

model PreGeneratedQuestion {
  id               String    @id
  questionType     String
  system           String?
  conditionId      String?
  medicalContentId String?

  questionData     Json
  generatedAt      DateTime  @default(now())
  usedAt           DateTime?
  usedBy           String?

  // Quality scoring (Phase 3)
  qualityScore      Float? // AI-assessed quality 0-100
  conditionAccuracy Float? // How well question tests the linked condition 0-1
  contentRelevance  Float? // How relevant to PANCE content 0-1
  distracorQuality  Float? // Quality of wrong answer options 0-1

  // Validation tracking
  validatedAt      DateTime? // When human validated
  validatedBy      String? // Admin userId who validated
  validationStatus String    @default("pending") // 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'needs_revision'
  validationNotes  String? // Admin notes on quality

  // Usage statistics
  timesServed    Int    @default(0) // How many times served to users
  timesCorrect   Int    @default(0) // Times answered correctly
  timesIncorrect Int    @default(0) // Times answered incorrectly
  avgTimeMs      Int? // Average time to answer
  flagCount      Int    @default(0) // Number of times flagged by users
  flagRate       Float? // flagCount / timesServed

  // Duplicate detection
  semanticHash       String? // Hash for duplicate detection
  similarQuestionIds String[] @default([]) // IDs of similar questions

  // FK relations
  Condition      Condition?      @relation(fields: [conditionId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  MedicalContent MedicalContent? @relation(fields: [medicalContentId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  @@index([conditionId])
  @@index([medicalContentId])
  @@index([difficulty])
  @@index([generatedAt])
  @@index([questionType])
  @@index([system])
  @@index([usedAt])
  @@index([validationStatus])
  @@index([qualityScore])
  @@index([flagRate])
}

model QuestionAttempt {
  id                 String   @id
  userId             String
  questionId         String
  questionType       String?
  system             String?
  conditionId        String?
  mode               String?
  wasCorrect         Boolean  @default(false)
  isRankedAttempt    Boolean  @default(false)
  // Time on question: timeSpentMs = session/attempt APIs; durationMs = drill submit (telemetry)
  timeSpentMs        Int? // Time spent on question in milliseconds (questions/attempt, review)
  answerChangedCount Int? // Number of times answer was changed before submission
  createdAt          DateTime @default(now())

  // Rolling 360 Support
  isMainSession    Boolean @default(false) // True for Main Session mode only
  systemNormalized String? // Pre-computed normalized system name for fast aggregation

  // Phase 3: Telemetry Injection (Cognitive Fidelity)
  telemetryJson   Json?   // Detailed behavioral telemetry: MVRT, time_to_first_interaction, rapid_guess, etc.
  durationMs      Int?    // Total duration from display to submission (ms); set by drill submit-review
  selectedAnswer  String? // Answer choice selected (e.g. A, B, C, D) for drill/analytics

  // Metacognition: implicit confidence (0-1) from behavior for Calibration / Illusion of Competence
  implicitConfidence Float? // 0-1; high+right=mastered, high+wrong=dangerous, low+right=lucky, low+wrong=unconfident

  @@index([isRankedAttempt])
  @@index([mode])
  @@index([questionId])
  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@index([system])
  @@index([userId, isMainSession, createdAt])
  // === 7 ADDITIONAL INDEXES FOR ROLLING 360 ANALYTICS ===
  // These indexes enable:
  // 1. Fast DESC sorting for latest attempts
  // 2. System-specific performance queries
  // 3. Correctness-based filtering
  // 4. Question type analytics
  // 5. Mode-based performance tracking
  // 6. Global time-series queries
  // 7. Condition-specific tracking
  @@index([userId, isMainSession, createdAt(sort: Desc)], map: "QuestionAttempt_userId_isMainSession_createdAt_desc_idx")
  @@index([userId, systemNormalized, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, wasCorrect, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, questionType, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, mode, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt(sort: Desc)])
  @@index([conditionId, createdAt])
}

// =============================================================================
// CARD: FSRS v6 Compliant Model (Replaces SRSItem)
// Matches ts-fsrs Card interface exactly for seamless integration
// =============================================================================
model Card {
  id         String @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String
  questionId String // Connects to Question/MedicalContent

  // FSRS v6 State (matches ts-fsrs Card interface)
  due            DateTime // Next review date
  stability      Float     @default(0.0) // S - memory stability in days
  difficulty     Float     @default(0.0) // D - item difficulty [0-10]
  elapsed_days   Float     @default(0.0) // Days since card creation
  scheduled_days Float     @default(0.0) // Days until next review
  reps           Int       @default(0) // Total review count
  lapses         Int       @default(0) // Number of failed reviews
  state          Int       @default(0) // 0=New, 1=Learning, 2=Review, 3=Relearning
  last_review    DateTime? // Timestamp of last review

  // Metadata
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  // Relations
  user User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([userId, questionId])
  @@index([userId, due]) // CRITICAL: Fast "Due Today" queries
  @@index([userId, state]) // Filter by learning state
  @@index([questionId]) // Lookup by content
}

model SemanticCache {
  id             String   @id
  queryText      String
  queryEmbedding String?
  questionType   String
  system         String?
  difficulty     String?
  cachedQuestion Json
  generatedAt    DateTime @default(now())

  qualityScore   Float    @default(0)

  @@index([lastUsedAt])
  @@index([queryText(ops: raw("gin_trgm_ops"))], type: Gin)
  @@index([questionType])
  @@index([system])
}

/// Identified knowledge gap (e.g. from OSCE differential-diagnosis failure) for Tutor to target.
model ConceptGap {
  id         String   @id @default(uuid())
  userId     String
  system     String   // e.g. "Cardiology", "Pulmonary"
  sourceType String   // e.g. "osce"
  sourceId   String?  // e.g. OsceResult id
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
  User       User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([userId, system])
}

model UserStatistics {
  id     String @id @default(cuid())
  userId String @unique

  // Overall Performance
  totalQuestions     Int    @default(0)
  correctAnswers     Int    @default(0)
  avgTimePerQuestion Float?

  // System-level breakdown (stored as JSON for flexibility)
  systemStats Json?

  // Behavioral Patterns
  rushedSystems       String[] @default([])
  overthinkingSystems String[] @default([])

  // Diagnosis Bias (conditions frequently over-selected as wrong answers)
  diagnosisBias Json?

  // Strengths/Weaknesses (auto-calculated)
  strongestSystems String[] @default([])
  weakestSystems   String[] @default([])

  // Time tracking
  peakStudyHours   Int[]  @default([])
  avgSessionLength Float?

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  User User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId])
}

  @@index([medicalContentId])

}

// =============================================================================
// NEW MODELS FOR "SECOND CHANCE" SYSTEM
// =============================================================================

model QuestionVariant {
  id             String @id @default(cuid())
  baseQuestionId String // Link to original Question or conditionId
  variantType    String // "rephrased" | "different_distractors" | "different_vitals" | "different_scenario"

  // Question content
  vignette      String?
  question      String
  options       String[] // 4 answer choices
  correctAnswer String
  explanation   String

  // Metadata
  difficulty String  @default("medium")
  taskType   String? // "diagnosis" | "treatment" | "mechanism" | "next_step"

  usedByUsers String[] // UserIds who have seen this variant
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([baseQuestionId])
  @@index([taskType])
}

model UserTopicProgress {
  id          String @id @default(cuid())
  userId      String
  conditionId String // MedicalContent ID
  taskType    String // "diagnosis" | "treatment" | "mechanism" | "workup" | "prevention"

  // FSRS state per topic
  stability      Float     @default(1.0)
  difficulty     Float     @default(5.0)
  lastReviewDate DateTime?
  nextReviewDate DateTime?
  state          Int       @default(0) // 0=New, 1=Learning, 2=Review, 3=Relearning
  reps           Int       @default(0)
  lapses         Int       @default(0)

  // Track variants used
  variantsUsed String[] // QuestionVariant IDs already shown

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  User User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([userId, conditionId, taskType])
  @@index([userId, nextReviewDate])
}

model StudyRecommendation {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId      String
  type        String // "drill_session" | "review" | "new_topic"
  topic       String // e.g., "Cardiology", "Beta Blockers" (or conditionId)
  reason      String // "Fading memory", "Low accuracy"
  priority    String // "high", "medium", "low"
  status      String // "pending", "completed", "dismissed"
  data        Json? // Optional context (e.g., list of question IDs)
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  completedAt DateTime?

  User User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId, status])
  @@index([userId, priority])
}

/// Drug-drug interactions (self-referential many-to-many)
model DrugInteraction {
  id                String   @id
  drugId1           String
  drugId2           String
  severity          String // 'major', 'moderate', 'minor'
  mechanism         String?
  clinicalEffect    String?
  management        String?
  isContraindicated Boolean  @default(false)
  createdAt         DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime @default(now())
  Drug1             Drug     @relation("DrugInteraction1", fields: [drugId1], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  Drug2             Drug     @relation("DrugInteraction2", fields: [drugId2], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([drugId1, drugId2])
  @@index([drugId1])
  @@index([drugId2])
  @@index([severity])
}

/// Condition-to-condition relationships (differentials, complications, etc.)
model ConditionRelation {
  id              String    @id
  conditionId1    String
  conditionId2    String
  relationType    String // 'differential', 'complication', 'precursor', 'associated'
  bidirectional   Boolean   @default(true)
  clinicalContext String?
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now())
  Condition1      Condition @relation("ConditionRelation1", fields: [conditionId1], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  Condition2      Condition @relation("ConditionRelation2", fields: [conditionId2], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([conditionId1, conditionId2, relationType])
  @@index([conditionId1])
  @@index([conditionId2])
  @@index([relationType])
}

enum CohortRole {
  ADMIN
  MEMBER
}

enum UserRole {
  USER
  VIEWER
  EDITOR
  APPROVER
  ADMIN
  SUPERADMIN
}

/// User Lifecycle: Student vs. practicing PA (distinct from RBAC UserRole)
enum UserLifecycleRole {
  STUDENT
  PRACTICING_PA
}

/// Training phase for PA program progression
enum TrainingPhase {
  DIDACTIC
  CLINICAL
  GRADUATED
}

/// Bloom's taxonomy / cognitive demand level for questions
enum CognitiveLevel {
  LEVEL_1_RECALL
  LEVEL_2_CONCEPT
  LEVEL_3_VIGNETTE
}

enum WordleStatus {
  playing
  won
  lost
}

enum SessionType {
  MAIN // Only these count for FSRS optimization
  CRAM // Practice mode - excluded from stats
  RAPID_RECALL // Quick review - excluded from stats
}

// ============================================================================
// Sprint 4: Enhanced Session Analytics
// ============================================================================

model StudySession {
  id        String    @id
  userId    String
  startedAt DateTime  @default(now())
  endedAt   DateTime?

  // Core metrics
  totalQuestions     Int   @default(0)
  correctAnswers     Int   @default(0)
  accuracy           Float @default(0)
  totalTimeMs        Int   @default(0)
  avgTimePerQuestion Int?

  // Streak data
  bestStreak    Int    @default(0)
  finalStreak   Int    @default(0)
  avgStreak     Float?
  errorClusters Int    @default(0) // Count of 3+ errors in 5 questions

  // Momentum metrics
  peakMomentum  Float? // Highest momentum reached (0-100)
  avgMomentum   Float?
  momentumTrend String? // 'peaked' | 'rising' | 'steady' | 'cooling' | 'cold'

  // Behavioral signals
  totalAnswerChanges    Int    @default(0)
  helpfulChanges        Int    @default(0) // Changed to correct
  harmfulChanges        Int    @default(0) // Changed to wrong
  firstInstinctAccuracy Float? // Accuracy if never changed answers

  // Confidence calibration
  avgInferredConfidence  Float? // Average behavioral confidence
  highConfidenceAccuracy Float? // Accuracy when high confidence
  lowConfidenceAccuracy  Float? // Accuracy when low confidence
  calibrationScore       Float? // How well confidence matches accuracy

  // Time pressure analysis
  questionsUnderPar  Int    @default(0)
  questionsOverPar   Int    @default(0)
  rushingAccuracy    Float? // Accuracy when under 60% of par time
  deliberateAccuracy Float? // Accuracy when over par time

  // Fatigue indicators
  early10Accuracy Float? // First 10 questions accuracy
  late10Accuracy  Float? // Last 10 questions accuracy
  staminaFade     Float? // Difference (negative = fatigue)

  // PANCE distribution
  distributionScore Float? // How closely session matched PANCE blueprint
  systemsCovered    String[] @default([]) // Systems practiced
  systemsTargeted   String[] @default([]) // Explicitly selected systems for the session

  // Predicted score
  predictedScore  Int? // Estimated PANCE score (200-800)
  scoreConfidence String? // 'low' | 'medium' | 'high'
  passLikelihood  Float? // 0-100%

  // Session settings
  sessionType String? // 'mixed' | 'focused' | 'review'
  mode        String? // 'drill' | 'review' | 'exam'
  focus       String? // 'new' | 'review' | 'missed'
  difficulty  String? // 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard' | 'random'

  // Metadata & Context (Step 7: Session Context Tracking)
  deviceType     String? // 'mobile' | 'tablet' | 'desktop'
  browserName    String?
  screenSize     String? // e.g., "1920x1080"
  connectionType String? // 'wifi' | 'cellular' | 'unknown'
  likelyContext  String? // 'commute' | 'home' | 'library' | 'unknown'
  isQuickSession Boolean @default(false) // < 10 questions
  wasInterrupted Boolean @default(false) // Large gaps detected

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  // New FK for deep relations
  User             User?             @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  SessionAnalytics SessionAnalytics?

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([userId, startedAt])
  @@index([startedAt])
}

// Sprint C - Step 8: Question Versioning

  // Editor info

  editedByEmail String? // User email for audit trail

  // Validation scores

  qualityScore    Int? // Overall quality score (0-100)

  // Timestamps

  @@unique([questionId, questionType, version])
  @@index([questionId, version])
  @@index([questionId, questionType])
  @@index([editedBy])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

/// NCCPA PANCE Blueprint configuration for exam distribution validation
model NCCPABlueprintConfig {
  id             String    @id @default(cuid())
  version        String    @unique // "2024", "2025", etc.
  isActive       Boolean   @default(false)
  weights        Json // {"Cardiovascular": 0.11, "Pulmonary": 0.09, ...}
  systemCount    Int
  effectiveDate  DateTime  @db.Date
  expirationDate DateTime? @db.Date
  createdAt      DateTime  @default(now())

  @@index([isActive])
}

/// Cache table for O(1) dashboard reads of Rolling 360 metrics
model UserRolling360Stats {
  id     String @id @default(cuid())
  userId String @unique

  // Rolling Aggregates (max 360)
  totalInWindow   Int      @default(0)
  correctInWindow Int      @default(0)
  accuracyPercent Decimal? @db.Decimal(5, 2)

  // System Breakdown (JSONB)
  // Format: {"Cardiovascular": {"total": 42, "correct": 35}, ...}
  systemStats Json @default("{}")

  // PANCE Score Prediction (200-800 scale)
  predictedScore  Int?
  scoreConfidence String? // 'collecting' | 'provisional' | 'confident'
  passLikelihood  Decimal? @db.Decimal(5, 2) // 0-100%

  // Blueprint Adherence
  blueprintAdherence      Decimal? @db.Decimal(5, 4) // 0-1 (cosine similarity)
  isValidExamDistribution Boolean  @default(false)

  // Weakness Detection
  weakestSystems   String[] @default([])
  strongestSystems String[] @default([])

  // Window Tracking
  windowStartAttemptId String? // Oldest attempt in window
  windowEndAttemptId   String? // Newest attempt in window
  windowStartTime      DateTime?
  windowEndTime        DateTime?

  // Circular Buffer Index
  currentSlotIndex Int @default(0) // 0-359

  // Metadata
  lastRecalculatedAt   DateTime @default(now())
  recalculationVersion Int      @default(1)
  createdAt            DateTime @default(now())

  User User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([predictedScore])
}

/// Circular buffer table for O(1) sliding window updates (360 slots per user)
model Rolling360Buffer {
  id        String @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String
  slotIndex Int // 0-359

  // Cached data from original QuestionAttempt
  attemptId        String? // FK to QuestionAttempt
  isCorrect        Boolean?
  systemNormalized String?
  answeredAt       DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([userId, slotIndex])
  @@index([userId, slotIndex])
}

// PersonalizedFSRSParams: Per-user FSRS optimization
model PersonalizedFSRSParams {
  id     String @id @default(cuid())
  userId String @unique

  // Optimized w[] array (21 parameters for FSRS v6)
  // Changed from Json to Float[] for direct Rust optimizer compatibility
  w Float[] // The 21 FSRS weights (v6) - Postgres array for performance

  // Optimization metadata
  sampleSize             Int? // Reviews used for optimization
  lastOptimizedAt        DateTime?
  improvementOverDefault Float? // % Brier score reduction
  optimizationIterations Int?

  // Per-system adjustments
  systemModifiers Json? // {"CV": {"stabilityMult": 1.1, "diffOffset": 0.2}}

  // Validation
  validationBrierScore Float? // Score on holdout set
  parameterBounds      Json? // Constraints used

  // Relations
  User User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([userId])
}

// =============================================================================
// ContentIndex: Global Search Index (OPTIONAL - For Hybrid Content Engine)
// Enables unified search across all content types without multiple JOINs
// Populate via background job or triggers for 2,195+ content items
// =============================================================================
model ContentIndex {
  id         String  @id @default(cuid())
  entityId   String // ID of Drug, Condition, Anatomy, etc.
  entityType String // "Drug", "Condition", "Anatomy", "Symptom", "ECGPattern"
  title      String // Searchable title/name
  body       String  @db.Text // Full-text searchable content
  panceYield Int? // PANCE priority score
  system     String? // Organ system (CV, PULM, etc.)

  // Search optimization
  searchVector String? @db.Text // Optional: For PostgreSQL full-text search

  // Metadata
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([entityType, entityId])
  @@index([title])
  @@index([entityType, panceYield])
  @@index([system])
}

// src/lib/drugSearch.ts
// Search functionality for pharmacological agents and treatments

import type { DrugEntry, DrugSearchResult, DrugSearchFilters } from '../../pharm/drugTypes';
import { BRAND_NAME_MAP } from '../../lib/drugBrandNames';
import { drugService } from '@/services/domain';

// ============================================================================
// TYPES
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Drug data structure from Prisma database
 * Matches the Drug model from schema.prisma
 */
export interface DrugData {
  id: string;
  genericName: string;
  brandName: string | null;
  drugClass: string[];
  mechanismOfAction: string | null;
  indications: string[];
  contraindications: string[];
  sideEffects: string[];
  interactions: string[];
  dosing: string | null;
  displayName: string | null;
  aliases: string[];
  tags: string[];
  isHighYield: boolean;
  clinicalNotes: string | null;
  antidote: string | null;
  metabolism: string | null;
  elimination: string | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}
